### [68] [Adjacency-Based Spectral Proxy Control of Mobile Communication Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13616)
*Mariana del Castillo,Federico Larroca*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出A-Fiedler控制器，通过将Fiedler梯度控制器的图嵌入组件替换为邻接矩阵主导特征向量（即A-Fiedler嵌入），实现异构移动Agent网络中通信Agent的分布式在线重定位。该方法在无需Fiedler向量分布式估计（需要无界通信轮次收敛）的情况下，实现了与经典Fiedler梯度控制器相当的性能，并在有限通信轮次的分布式估计下表现出更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构移动Agent网络中，需要在线重定位通信Agent以维持任务Agent的网络连通性。代数连通度（algebraic connectivity）是常用的替代目标函数，但控制它依赖Fiedler向量，而Fiedler向量的分布式估计需要无界通信轮次才能收敛，难以满足实时控制需求。因此需要寻找更容易分布式估计的替代图嵌入。

Method: 首先识别出Fiedler梯度控制器可分解为局部交互规则和图嵌入两个组件。基于此结构分解，提出A-Fiedler控制器，将Fiedler嵌入替换为邻接矩阵的主导特征向量（图嵌入中常用的latent geometry表示），该嵌入更适合在局部通信约束下进行分布式实现。通过与经典Fiedler梯度控制器的对比实验评估性能。

Result: 在无通信约束场景下，A-Fiedler与经典Fiedler梯度控制器性能相当；在分布式估计场景下，A-Fiedler表现出更强的鲁棒性。具体而言，在相同通信轮次下，Fiedler梯度控制器可能收敛到不连通的配置，而A-Fiedler能维持网络性能。

Conclusion: A-Fiedler为分布式网络控制提供了一条更简单的路径，在保持与Fiedler梯度控制器相当性能的同时，显著降低了分布式实现的复杂度，提升了对有限通信资源的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We consider a heterogeneous mobile-agent network composed of uncontrolled task agents and controllable communication agents. The objective is to reposition communication agents online as task agents move. Since throughput-based objectives are generally unsuitable for real-time control, spectral graph metrics such as algebraic connectivity are commonly adopted as surrogate objectives. However, controlling algebraic connectivity relies on the eigenvector corresponding to the second-smallest eigenvalue of a graph's Laplacian matrix (i.e., the Fiedler vector), whose distributed estimation requires an unbounded number of communication rounds to converge.
  In this work, we identify a structural decomposition of this Fiedler-gradient controller into a local interaction rule and a graph embedding component, suggesting the use of alternative embeddings that are easier to estimate distributively than the Fiedler vector. As a particular instance, we propose A-Fiedler, which replaces the Fiedler embedding with the dominant eigenvector of the adjacency matrix, commonly used as a graph embedding of nodes into a latent geometry. This representation is more naturally suited for distributed implementation under local communication constraints.
  We evaluate A-Fiedler against the classical Fiedler-gradient controller. Results show comparable network performance in the absence of communication constraints and improved robustness under distributed estimation. For instance, under the same number of communication rounds, the Fielder-gradient may even converge to disconnected configurations whereas our proposition maintains performance. We believe our contribution provides a simpler path toward distributed network control.

</details>


### [69] [hint$^2$: Hierarchical World Models for Inference-Time Temporal Logic Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13678)
*Moritz Zoellner,Anastasios Manganaris,Ahmed H. Qureshi,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出hint²，一种在推理时通过分层世界模型引导短期行为策略满足复杂LTL规范的方法。高层模型预测动作引起的原子命题转移以推进LTL自动机，低层动力学模型预测即时状态演化以进行局部安全引导，在CALVIN基准和真实UR5e机械臂上均取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大规模语言条件策略在机器人学习上取得进展，但仍难以处理时间结构和安全约束。LTL（线性时序逻辑）能表达复杂的非马尔可夫指令，但现代策略生成短视界动作块并闭环重规划，而几乎所有LTL规范都是基于长视界轨迹评估的，两者之间存在根本性不匹配，导致引导学习型操作策略满足LTL约束成为一个挑战。

Method: hint²利用分层世界模型在推理时引导短视界策略。其核心思想是使用两个不同抽象层次的引导目标：高层世界模型预测未来动作引发的任务相关原子命题转移，用以引导策略在LTL自动机中的进度；低层动力学模型预测即时状态演化，用于局部安全引导。两个目标协同作用，将短视界行动引导到满足长视界LTL规范的方向上。

Result: hint²克服了现有LTL引导扩散方法的局限，在CALVIN基准上优于现有的推理时引导方法，并能比语言条件替代方法更优雅地完成具有复杂活性（liveness）和安全约束的指令。此外，在真实UR5e机械臂上成功处理了复杂的指令。

Conclusion: hint²通过分层世界模型成功弥合了短视界策略与长视界LTL规范之间的鸿沟，为在推理阶段将形式化时序约束注入学习型策略提供了一种有效且实用的方案，并在仿真与真实机器人上均得到验证。

Abstract: A central goal of robot learning is to enable robots to execute rich instructions specified at runtime. Large-scale language-conditioned policies have made substantial progress toward this goal, yet still struggle with temporal structure and safety constraints. Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) provides a powerful language to express complex, non-Markovian instructions. However, guiding learned manipulation policies toward LTL satisfaction remains challenging because modern policies generate short-horizon action chunks and replan in closed loop, while almost all LTL specifications are evaluated over long-horizon trajectories. In this paper, we introduce hint$^2$, a method for guiding short-horizon policies toward satisfying complex LTL specifications at inference time using hierarchical world models. Our key idea is to derive two separate guidance objectives using each world model's abstraction level. A high-level model predicts future action-induced transitions in task-relevant atomic propositions to guide progress through the LTL automaton, while a low-level dynamics model predicts immediate state evolution for accurate local safety guidance. Our results show that hint$^2$ overcomes the limitations of current LTL-guided diffusion methods, outperforms existing inference-time steering methods in CALVIN, and successfully completes instructions with complex liveness and safety constraints more elegantly than language-conditioned alternatives. Finally, we demonstrate that hint$^2$ can handle complex instructions on a real UR5e manipulator.

</details>


### [70] [Graph-MambaNav: Spatial-Temporal Graph Mamba Leveraging Object-Relation Knowledge for Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13723)
*Leyuan Sun,Genxin Chen,Linwei Ye,Yan Zhang,Xi Kan,Yanfei Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Graph-MambaNav，一种目标感知的空间-时间图编码框架，通过基于LLM先验的目标相关性启发式节点排序，结合Graph-Mamba的序列化机制与Mamba时序建模，在AI2-THOR和RoboTHOR上实现更好的目标导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的物体导航方法虽然引入了目标感知的特征或注意力，但保持置换不变性，缺乏对信息传播顺序的显式控制，难以建模目标依赖的重要性和长程依赖关系。因此需要一种能够显式控制节点优先级的图编码机制。

Method: 提出Graph-MambaNav框架：(1) 利用LLM提取常识性物体关系先验，初始化节点排序和边权重；(2) 基于目标相关性对物体进行启发式排序，使更重要物体更晚处理以聚合更丰富上下文；(3) 空间模块结合局部消息传递与Graph-Mamba的选择性扫描；(4) 时序模块利用Mamba对物体级时间顺序进行序列建模，选择性聚合长程历史上下文。

Result: 在AI2-THOR和RoboTHOR基准上取得了改进的导航性能并具备良好的泛化性，同时在真实机器人部署实验中进一步验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式节点优先级排序结合LLM常识先验与Mamba序列建模，能够有效增强目标依赖的长程时空推理能力，是改善目标导航的关键机制。

Abstract: Object-goal navigation requires an agent to reason over object relationships and prioritize target-relevant objects for efficient decision making in unseen environments. While existing graph-based methods incorporate target-awareness at the feature or attention level, they remain permutation-invariant and lack an explicit mechanism to control information propagation order, limiting their ability to model target-dependent importance and long-range dependencies. In contrast, Graph-Mamba highlights that node prioritization through sequence ordering is critical for effective global reasoning. In this work, we investigate the node prioritization mechanism in Graph-Mamba and study its role in object navigation. We propose Graph-MambaNav, a target-aware spatial-temporal graph encoding framework that introduces a heuristic ordering over objects based on their relevance to the target, allowing more informative objects to be processed later to aggregate richer context. Both node ordering and edge weights are initialized from LLM-derived commonsense object relationships, providing a unified prior for structured reasoning. A spatial module integrates local message passing with global GraphMamba-based selective scanning, while a temporal module applies Mamba-based sequence modeling over object-wise temporal orders, allowing selective aggregation of historical context for long-range temporal reasoning. Experiments on AI2-THOR and RoboTHOR demonstrate improved navigation performance with generalization, and additional real-world robot deployment further validates the effectiveness of our proposed approach.

</details>


### [71] [Knowledge-Data-Dual-Driven Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Vehicle Control in Mixed Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13878)
*Jie Fang,Wei Zheng,Mengyun Xu,Eui-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出KDDRL（知识-数据双驱动强化学习）框架，通过条件深度生成模型生成意图感知的未来轨迹、知识-数据双驱动范式引导安全探索，以及耦合模块实现连续跟驰与离散换道的异步多时间尺度优化，从而解决混合交通中自动驾驶决策的意图不确定性、非平稳性与混合动作空间三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合交通中自动驾驶决策面临三个相互关联的挑战：(1) 基于物理先验的强化学习模型无法捕捉潜在的交互相机意图和多样化的驾驶员行为，限制了主动推理能力；(2) 周围车辆的突然机动造成非平稳性，导致长尾安全事件探索不足；(3) 连续跟驰与离散换道动作具有不同的时间尺度，导致混合动作空间使统一强化学习训练不稳定。

Method: 提出知识-数据双驱动强化学习（KDDRL）框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 条件深度生成模型用于合成意图感知的未来轨迹，将被动感知转化为主动预测状态；(2) 知识-数据双驱动范式作用于预测状态，融合概率数据驱动洞察与物理约束，引导穿越安全关键场景的安全探索；(3) 耦合模块将意图感知轨迹和物理约束压缩为紧凑的共享嵌入，实现连续跟驰与离散换道的异步多时间尺度优化，同时保持互信息。

Result: 在基于数据集校准的仿真评估中，KDDRL有效处理了意图不确定性，加速了训练收敛，并在安全性、效率和舒适性方面优于传统基线方法。

Conclusion: KDDRL通过知识-数据双驱动框架成功解决了混合交通场景下自动驾驶决策面临的意图不确定性、非平稳性和混合动作空间三大问题，验证了融合生成式预测、物理约束与多时间尺度耦合优化的有效性，为混合交通中的智能驾驶决策提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In mixed traffic, decision-making for autonomous vehicles (AVs) confronts three interrelated challenges. First, physics-based priors incorporated into reinforcement learning (RL) models fail to capture latent interactive vehicle intentions and diverse driver behaviors, limiting the proactive reasoning capabilities. Second, abrupt maneuvers by surrounding vehicles cause non-stationarity, leaving long-tail safety events under-explored. Third, hybrid action spaces destabilize unified RL training due to the different temporal scales of continuous car-following and discrete lane-changing maneuvers. To address these issues, we propose Knowledge-Data Dual-driven Reinforcement Learning (KDDRL). First, a conditional deep generative model synthesizes intention-aware future trajectories, converting passive perception into proactive predictive states. Second, a knowledge-data dual-driven paradigm operates on these predictive states, fusing probabilistic data-driven insights with physical constraints to guide safe exploration through safety-critical scenarios. Third, a coupling module compresses both intention-aware trajectories and physical constraints into compact shared embeddings. This unified representation enables asynchronous multi-timescale optimization of continuous car-following and discrete lane-changing while preserving mutual information. Evaluations on dataset-calibrated simulations demonstrate that KDDRL effectively handles intention uncertainty, accelerates training convergence, and outperforms conventional baseline methods in terms of safety, efficiency, and comfort.

</details>


### [72] [Ontology-Grounded World Models for Failure Diagnosis and Closed-Loop Repair in Physical AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13901)
*Kailin Wang,Haoxiang Jie,Yaoyuan Yan,Jiacheng Zhou,Zhiyou Heng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Onto-EV-WM，一种在EV-WM之上构建的、基于本体的诊断与验证门控修正接口。它通过任务局部TBox定义实体类型与谓词签名，将预测/模拟状态映射到ABox，确定性规则保留缺失谓词及其参数，并分配修正路径标签。在PointMaze和LIBERO基准上，Onto-EV-WM在保持94%成功率的同时，将平均最终状态距离从0.90573降至0.61177，在LIBERO-Plus注册表上达到85.00%的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EV-WM通过特征分数和事件分数表示候选质量，但这些分数未显式记录未满足的任务谓词、可用的修正机制路径标签，以及修正后的接受结果。缺乏对这些信息的显式表示，导致诊断和纠错难以系统化。

Method: Onto-EV-WM是一个叠加在EV-WM之上的本体驱动诊断与验证门控修正接口，而非替代世界模型架构。它包含：(1)任务局部TBox，定义实体类型、谓词签名和约束；(2)源码特定的接地映射，将预测或模拟观察到的状态映射到任务ABox；(3)确定性规则，在分配路径标签时保留缺失谓词及其参数。学习/启发式提议者与符号接口保持分离，由原生任务谓词决定接受，有界协议决定验证失败是否重试。

Result: 在PointMaze评估中，EV-WM与Onto-EV-WM均报告94%成功率，平均最终状态距离分别为0.90573和0.61177，单独预算的搜索达到100%成功率。在LIBERO-Goal上，完整配置在seed 0上报告93.8%的修正窗口成功率，在四个评估采样种子上为94.05±0.30%。在固定的10,030任务LIBERO-Plus注册表上，Onto-EV-WM成功完成8,526个任务（85.00%），各套件成功率为LIBERO-10 65.98%、LIBERO-Goal 91.39%、LIBERO-Object 91.38%、LIBERO-Spatial 91.38%。

Conclusion: Onto-EV-WM通过本体化的显式诊断和验证门控修正，在不改变世界模型架构的前提下提升了纠错精度，显著改善了最终状态距离。但需注意：本体的独立因果贡献未单独测量，且尚未在真实机器人上进行恢复评估。

Abstract: EV-WM represents candidate quality with feature and event scores, but these scores do not explicitly record an unmet task predicate, a route label for an available correction mechanism, or a post-correction acceptance result. We present Onto-EV-WM, an ontology-grounded diagnosis and verification-gated correction interface layered above EV-WM rather than a replacement world-model architecture. The implemented task-local TBox defines entity types, predicate signatures, and constraints; source-specific grounding maps predicted or simulator-observed states to task ABoxes; and deterministic rules retain each missing predicate and its arguments when assigning a route label. Learned or heuristic proposers remain separate from this symbolic interface; native task predicates determine acceptance, and the bounded protocol determines whether a failed verification is retried. In the aligned PointMaze evaluation, EV-WM and Onto-EV-WM both report 94% success, with mean final-state distances of 0.90573 and 0.61177, respectively; the separately budgeted search reaches 100% success. On LIBERO-Goal, the ontology represents failed task conditions as typed records, retains their predicate arguments, and associates them with the declared source/joint correction route and predicate-gated acceptance; the complete configuration reports 93.8% corrected-window success on seed 0 and 94.05 +- 0.30% across four evaluation-sampling seeds. On the fixed 10,030-task LIBERO-Plus registry, Onto-EV-WM succeeds on 8,526 tasks (85.00%), with suite-level success rates of 65.98% for LIBERO-10, 91.39% for LIBERO-Goal, and 91.38% for both LIBERO-Object and LIBERO-Spatial. These numbers report the performance of the complete ontology-grounded configurations under the tested simulator protocols; an ontology-only causal share is not measured separately, and real-robot recovery is not evaluated.

</details>


### [73] [Communication in modular robotic motor control: Bilateral controllers under realistic constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13904)
*Jingwen Li,Levin Kuhlmann,Jason Friedman,Gideon Kowadlo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文受大脑双侧半球组织启发，提出一种由两个GRU模块组成、通过可学习的延迟信道连接的回环控制器，并在可微分的双臂肌肉骨骼模拟器中进行端到端训练。实验表明，该模块化架构在到达和保持任务中显著优于容量匹配的单体架构；与无通信的模块化控制器相比，学习到的半球间通信能改善末端精度、降低非零延迟下的能量消耗并减少肌肉共收缩，为生物启发的模块化控制在噪声与能量约束下的鲁棒运动提供了实用路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌肉骨骼系统中的机器人运动控制需要在信号依赖噪声和能量代价约束下实现快速精确的运动与鲁棒的姿态稳定。模块化控制器有望在这些竞争性需求之间进行分配，但在现实约束下其是否优于单体架构仍不明确，且模块间通信如何影响控制策略也缺乏研究。

Method: 提出一个由两个GRU模块（模拟大脑双侧半球）组成的回环控制器，模块间通过一个可学习的、具有延迟的信道进行通信。控制器在可微分的双臂肌肉骨骼模拟器中端到端训练，并针对到达和保持两类任务进行验证，与容量匹配的单体控制器以及无通信的模块化控制器进行对比。

Result: 模块化架构在到达和保持任务中大幅优于容量匹配的单体基线；与无通信的模块化控制器相比，学习到的半球间通信重塑了控制方案：提高了末端精度、在非零延迟情况下降低了能量消耗，并减少了肌肉共收缩。

Conclusion: 生物启发的模块化控制器为机器人在噪声和能量约束下的鲁棒运动提供了一条实用途径，而模块间通信提供了一种在精度、稳定性和驱动代价之间调节权衡的机制。

Abstract: Robotic motor control in musculoskeletal systems requires fast, accurate movement and robust postural stabilization under signal-dependent noise (where motor command variance scales with command magnitude) and energetic cost. Modular controllers can distribute these competing demands across interacting submodules, but it remains unclear whether they outperform monolithic architectures under realistic constraints, and how inter-module communication shapes the resulting strategy. Inspired by the bilateral hemispheric organization of the brain, we introduce a recurrent controller of two GRU-based modules connected by a learnable, delayed inter-hemispheric channel, trained end-to-end in a differentiable two-arm musculoskeletal simulator. Across reaching and holding tasks, the modular architecture substantially outperforms a capacity-matched monolithic baseline. Compared to a matched modular controller without communication, learned inter-hemispheric communication reshapes the solution: improved endpoint precision, lower energetic cost in non-zero-delay regimes, and reduced muscle co-contraction. Our findings show that for robotics, biologically inspired modular controllers offer a practical route to robust movement under noise and energetic constraints, with inter-module communication providing a mechanism to tune trade-offs between precision, stability, and actuation cost.

</details>


### [74] [BICPO-VLA: Behavior-Identified Continuation Preference Optimization for Smooth Asynchronous Vision-Language-Action Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13924)
*Ming Shang,Yuchen Huang,Jiaoyang Chen,Haoyuan Hu,Han Yu,Liping Song,Luyun Feng,Shuo Bao,Wei Dong,Xinzhou Wang,Fuchun Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BICPO-VLA是一种用于解决机器人动作生成中"请求到交接差距"问题的视觉语言动作模型框架。该差距源于三个耦合因素：请求时行为意图的模糊性、动作生成期间积累的物理状态漂移，以及新动作接管时的残余不兼容性。BICPO-VLA通过三步策略依次解决这些问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人动作生成过程中存在"请求到交接差距"（request-to-handoff gap），这一问题由三个相互耦合的来源造成：请求时行为意图的歧义、动作生成期间物理状态的漂移，以及新动作最终接管时的残余不兼容性。这一差距直接导致机器人动作衔接不流畅、执行不精确。

Method: BICPO-VLA采用了三步序贯方案：(1) 指令感知的因果历史编码器识别命令所支持的行为和当前任务进度；(2) 顺序Haar子空间生成将每个动作块分解为互补的成对脚手架系数和残差系数，实现两个专门的生成阶段后进行精确重构，通过在原始动作空间中减少迭代细化来缩短新动作块可用之前的机器人持续运动间隔；(3) BICPO将已知的退出动作滚动到实际交接状态，并在行为匹配的候选项中应用参考相对Flow-DPO，使生成的动作块适应剩余的请求到交接不匹配，同时不改变其预期行为。

Result: 通过减少原始动作空间的迭代细化来缩短动作生成间隔，并通过行为匹配的Flow-DPO微调实现动作块对剩余不匹配的适应，同时保持预期行为不变。

Conclusion: BICPO-VLA通过解耦三个耦合的问题来源并逐一定制解决方案，系统地缩小了请求到交接的差距，提升了机器人动作生成的连贯性和精确性。

Abstract: The request-to-handoff gap has three coupled sources: ambiguity about the behavior intended at request time, physical-state drift accumulated during action generation, and residual incompatibility when the new action finally assumes control. BICPO-VLA addresses them in sequence. First, an instruction-aware causal history encoder identifies the behavior supported by the command and current task progress. Second, sequential Haar subspace generation decomposes each action chunk into complementary pairwise scaffold and residual coefficients, enabling two specialized generation stages followed by exact reconstruction. By reducing iterative refinement in the original action space, it shortens the interval over which the robot continues moving before the new chunk becomes available. Finally, BICPO rolls the known outgoing actions to the actual handoff state and applies reference-relative Flow-DPO among behaviorally matched candidates, adapting the generated chunk to the remaining request-to-handoff mismatch without changing its intended behavior.

</details>


### [75] [AdvDex: Learning Dexterous Manipulation from Human Demonstrations via Joint-Aligned Actions and Adversarial Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14028)
*Zhiyue Zhao,Jingyi Wu,Hairuo Liu,Mingyu Liu,Liyang Li,Hengdi Zhang,Tong He,Zhengxue Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AdvDex，一个统一的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）框架，通过OmniShare多模态人类演示数据集、JAAS标准动作表示（SE(3)腕部姿态+15个手指关节）和域对抗学习，实现人类与机器人灵巧操作的跨具身泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作是具身智能的基础能力，但扩展面临两大挑战：机器人演示数据收集成本高昂，且不同具身（机器人）的动作空间存在差异。此外，在异构数据上训练的策略容易将任务相关视觉线索与具身特有的外观信息纠缠在一起，限制跨具身泛化能力。

Method: AdvDex包含三个核心组件：1) OmniShare，一个大规模多模态人类操作演示数据集，提供高质量运动学监督和触觉测量，减少对机器人遥操作的依赖；2) Joint-Aligned Action Space (JAAS)，一个包含SE(3)腕部位姿和15个手指关节的规范动作表示，在功能上对齐人类手、灵巧机器人手和平行夹爪；3) 域对抗学习，用于减少学习到的视觉表示中的具身特有信息。

Result: 在手部动作预测和真实世界灵巧操作实验中，相比基线方法有一致提升；实现了有效的零样本人到机器人技能迁移、对未见物体和环境的泛化，以及数据高效的少样本适应。

Conclusion: AdvDex通过将人类演示与规范动作空间及域对抗视觉学习相结合，为解决灵巧操作中跨具身数据稀缺和泛化难题提供了统一有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation is a fundamental capability for embodied intelligence, but scaling it remains difficult because robot demonstrations are expensive to collect and action spaces vary across embodiments. Policies trained on heterogeneous data can also entangle task-relevant visual cues with embodiment-specific appearance, limiting cross-embodiment generalization. We present AdvDex, a unified Vision-Language-Action framework for learning dexterous manipulation from human and robot demonstrations. First, we introduce OmniShare, a large-scale multimodal dataset of human manipulation demonstrations that provides high-quality kinematic supervision and tactile measurements while reducing reliance on robot teleoperation. Second, we propose the Joint-Aligned Action Space (JAAS), a canonical action representation comprising an $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ wrist pose and 15 finger joints, thereby functionally aligning human hands, dexterous robot hands, and parallel grippers. Finally, we use domain-adversarial learning to reduce embodiment-specific information in the learned visual representation. Experiments on hand-action prediction and real-world dexterous manipulation show consistent improvements over baselines, effective zero-shot human-to-robot skill transfer, generalization to unseen objects and environments, and data-efficient few-shot adaptation.

</details>


### [76] [Demonstration of Space Robot Teleoperation over a Lossy and Delayed Network using ATMOS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14031)
*Inkyu Jang,Gregorio Marchesini,Nicola De Carli,Byeongjun Kim,Sunwoo Hwang,Dabin Kim,Elias Krantz,Youngkyoung Kong,Frank J. Jiang,Annika Wong,Pedro Roque,Prasetyo W. L. Sanjaya,Nicola Bastianello,Mani H. Dhullipalla,Karl H. Johansson,Hyungbo Shim,Dimos V. Dimarogonas,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文展示了ATMOS（多用途轨道系统自主测试平台）——一种平面航天器类机器人，用于在微重力类似条件下对制导与控制策略进行硬件在环评估。作者开发并实验验证了一种结合状态预测与轨迹跟踪控制的策略，以应对地面操作员与ATMOS系统之间时变的随机通信延迟，实现对接机动。演示包括首尔与斯德哥尔摩之间的长距离远程控制实验，引入了真实的洲际延迟与变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轨航天器遥操作面临显著的通信往返延迟，而地面上缺乏能够在微重力类似条件下对控制架构进行硬件在环验证的低成本测试平台，这限制了太空遥操作概念的鲁棒性验证。

Method: 开发了ATMOS平面航天器模拟机器人作为物理测试平台；设计并实现了一种将状态预测与轨迹跟踪控制相结合的控制架构，用于远程操控下的对接机动；通过韩国首尔与瑞典斯德哥尔摩之间的洲际远程控制实验来引入并测试真实的时变随机通信延迟。

Result: 实验成功验证了所提控制策略在真实洲际通信延迟条件下能够完成对接机动，证明了ATMOS平台能够支持快速、可靠且经济高效的航天器遥操作概念测试，为微重力类似环境下的在轨操作鲁棒验证迈出了第一步。

Conclusion: ATMOS平台为航天器遥操作概念提供了一种低成本、可复现的硬件在环测试途径；结合状态预测的控制策略在时变随机通信延迟下表现良好，为未来在轨操作的地面预验证奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: We present a demonstration showcasing the Autonomy Testbed for Multi-purpose Orbiting Systems (ATMOS), a planar spacecraft-analog robot designed for hardware-in-the-loop evaluation of guidance and control strategies in microgravity-like conditions. Using ATMOS as the physical test platform, we investigate the design, analysis, and performance evaluation of control architectures for remotely operated spacecraft under round-trip communication delays. In this work, we develop and experimentally validate a control strategy that combines state prediction and trajectory tracking control to perform a docking maneuver, accounting for time-varying random communication latency between ground operators and the ATMOS system. The demonstration includes a long-distance remote control experiment between Seoul and Stockholm, introducing realistic intercontinental delays and variability. The results highlight the capability of ATMOS to support rapid, reliable, and cost-effective testing of spacecraft teleoperation concepts, establishing a first step toward robust validation of on-orbit operations in microgravity-like environments.

</details>


### [77] [Evolve Vision-Language-Action Model into an Agent with On-the-fly Tool-use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14047)
*Yi Ding,Yanzhao Yu,Xili Dai,Xianbiao Qi,Peiwen Sun,Xueqian Wang,Xiangyu Yue,Jianan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ART框架，将端到端VLA模型与工具调用（agentic tool-use）结合，通过注入现成的工具模块（低层视觉、高层affordance、具身增强）来降低动作解空间的复杂度，从而提升跨任务泛化能力并降低数据依赖。仅用30K规模数据集即达到比主流基线高20%的成功率，验证了模块化工具有效促进高效训练、轻量部署和可扩展集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有vanilla VLA模型面临两大挑战：1）动作解空间为连续全空间，导致跨任务泛化能力差；2）依赖大规模数据训练，数据获取成本高。作者希望通过工具注入的方式分解动作空间，从而同时解决泛化性和数据效率问题。

Method: 提出ART（Agentic Robot with Tool-use）框架，核心是"工具注入"：将现成的工具模块（低层视觉、高层affordance、具身增强三类）注入任意VLA模型中，使模型能够调用工具来分解复杂任务。具体包括：1）构建30K规模的工具使用轨迹与动作演示数据集（远小于基线）；2）设计长轨迹工具使用推理的训练方案，面向挑战性环境。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界任务（如黑暗环境中的抓取放置、新颖视角）中，ART相比主流基线取得了20%更高的成功率。实验证实模块化工具利用能带来更高效的训练、轻量级部署以及新工具的可扩展集成。

Conclusion: 基于agent的方法（工具调用）能显著增强VLA系统的鲁棒性、适应性和可扩展性，为VLA系统在复杂真实场景中的实际部署铺平了道路。

Abstract: This paper integrates end-to-end Visual-Language-Action (VLA) models with agentic tool-use to propose Agentic Robot with Tool-use (ART). ART is a tool-injection framework that tunes any VLA model to leverage off-the-shelf tool modules for low-level vision, high-level affordance, and embodiment enhancement. Compared to vanilla VLA models with a whole continuous action solution space, ART reduces the complexity of the action solution space through tool-use, which not only improves generalizability across different tasks but also reduces data dependency. To demonstrate the advantages (high generalizability and low data dependency) of this framework, we first built a dataset of 30K tool-use trajectories and action demonstrations, which is much smaller than those used by baseline methods. We then designed a training regimen for long-trajectory tool-use reasoning in challenging environments. Experiments show that ART achieves a 20% higher success rate than mainstream baselines on simulation and real-world tasks, such as pick-and-place in the dark at novel viewpoints. Empirical results highlight the benefits of an agent-based approach: modular tool utilization enables more efficient training, lightweight deployment, and scalable integration of new tools. This design fosters robustness, adaptability, and extensibility, paving the way for the practical deployment of VLA systems in complex real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [78] [FlatLab: A Unified Methodology Framework and Simulation-Based Benchmark for Robotic Manipulation of Flat Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14049)
*Xingyu Zhu,Wenshuo Han,Zhouyu Wang,Yuran Wang,Ruihai Wu,Hao Dong,Fan Tang,Hechang Chen,Hyung Jin Chang,Yixing Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的平面物体操作框架，将操作解耦为策略生成器和动作执行模块，并引入FlatLab仿真基准（支持高保真物理仿真、多模态数据收集和标准化评估），在未知物体和类别上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面物体（如纸张、布料、卡片等）的机器人操作极具挑战性，因为这类物体通常无法被抓取，且几何形状和材料差异巨大。现有方法依赖启发式预操作，且常在封闭环境中评估，泛化能力有限。

Method: 框架由两部分组成：(1) 策略生成器：通过模拟数据变换和对比学习，从物体点云中学习策略中心且物体不变的表示，预测合适的操作策略；(2) 动作执行模块：以预测策略为条件，将长时程操作分解为可复用的动作原语，并动态组合生成稳定轨迹。同时提出FlatLab仿真基准，提供多样刚性与可变形平面物体的高保真物理仿真、自动化多模态数据收集及标准化任务定义和评估协议。

Result: 在FlatLab上的实验表明，该方法能有效泛化到未见过的物体和类别，性能优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 通过策略与执行的解耦以及模拟环境中的系统性评测，该框架为平面物体操作提供了一种通用且可泛化的解决方案，FlatLab也为该领域提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation of flat objects is challenging due to the ungraspable configurations and strong variations in object geometry and material. Existing methods rely on heuristic pre-manipulation and are often evaluated in closed settings with limited generalization. We propose a unified framework that decouples the manipulation into a strategy generator and an action execution module. The strategy generator predicts appropriate manipulation strategies from object point clouds by learning strategy-centric, object-invariant representations via simulated data transformation and contrastive learning. Conditioned on the predicted strategy, the execution module decomposes long-horizon manipulation into reusable action primitives and dynamically composes them to generate stable trajectories. To enable systematic evaluation, we introduce FlatLab, a comprehensive simulation benchmark for robotic flat object manipulation. FlatLab provides high-fidelity physical simulation of diverse rigid and deformable flat objects, automated multi-modal data collection, and standardized task definitions and evaluation protocols. Experiments conducted in FlatLab demonstrate that our approach generalizes effectively to unseen objects and categories, outperforming existing baselines. The project page and the code are provided at https://flatlab-web.github.io/.

</details>


### [79] [PILOT: Privileged Imitation Learning for End-to-End Motion Planning of Autonomous UAVs under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14082)
*Qingrui Zhang,Feng Xue,Xiang Zhou,Chenghao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PILOT框架，一种基于视觉的端到端UAV运动规划方法，通过约束感知特权模仿学习，在部分可观测和动态约束环境下实现自主导航。该框架从最优控制专家中蒸馏规划策略，利用TCN时空感知融合模块缓解部分可观测性问题，并通过轨迹参数化层保证动态一致性。在四旋翼和固定翼仿真中性能媲美特权专家，计算开销降低80%以上，并成功完成室内外零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱环境中的自主导航受到部分可观测性和动态约束的阻碍，现有基于视觉的端到端方法难以可靠处理这些挑战。

Method: 1) 构建约束感知特权模仿学习框架，从计算密集的最优控制专家中蒸馏策略；2) 设计双目标损失函数，对学生策略进行安全性和动态需求正则化；3) 开发基于时间卷积网络(TCN)的时空感知融合模块，集成历史深度图像与里程计信息，推断任务相关的潜在上下文；4) 引入轨迹参数化层，将网络输出映射为结构化轨迹，支持连续性、动态一致性和障碍物软惩罚的显式训练。

Result: 在四旋翼和固定翼飞机的仿真中，PILOT达到了与特权专家相当的性能，同时计算开销降低超过80%。室内和室外的零样本部署实验成功，验证了规划器的实际可行性和跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: PILOT验证了约束感知特权模仿学习结合时空感知融合能有效解决部分可观测下的端到端运动规划问题，在保持性能的同时大幅降低计算开销，且具备良好的跨域零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in cluttered environments is hampered by partial observability and dynamic constraints. This paper presents PILOT, a constraint-aware privileged imitation learning framework for vision-based end-to-end UAV motion planning under partial observability. The framework distills planning strategies from a computationally intensive optimal control expert into a student policy regularized toward safety and dynamic requirements via a dual-objective loss function. To mitigate partial observability, a spatiotemporal perception fusion module using a Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) is developed to integrate historical depth images and odometry. This module infers task-relevant latent context from historical observations, enhancing spatial awareness beyond the instantaneous FOV without maintaining persistent map memory. A trajectory parameterization layer mapping network outputs to a structured trajectory, while enabling explicit continuity, dynamic-consistency, and obstacle soft penalties during training, encouraging constraint satisfaction for unseen observations without formal guarantees. Simulations on quadrotor and fixed-wing aircraft demonstrate that PILOT achieves performance comparable to the privileged expert while reducing computational overhead by over 80\%. Successful indoor and outdoor zero-shot deployment confirms the practical feasibility and cross-domain generalization of the planner.

</details>


### [80] [AgilePE: Autonomous UAV Pursuit-Evasion via Self-Play Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14135)
*Wenhao Tang,Tianyang Chen,Zhejun Cui,Boyuan An,Jiayu Chen,Ruize Zhang,Huidong Liu,Tianyue Wu,Qingmin Liao,Fei Gao,Yu Wang,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AgilePE，一个通过自博弈强化学习实现无人机自主追逃的完整系统。该系统将敏捷低层控制、竞争性策略优化与仿真到实机部署统一在同一框架中，策略直接映射机载状态观测到Collective Thrust and Body Rates (CTBR)指令，实现端到端敏捷机动。训练采用带优先级的虚构自博弈（PFSP）和多样化对手池来稳定优化，部署通过硬件对齐的仿真管线实现零样本迁移到真实四旋翼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主追逃是无人机领域的基础性挑战，需要在紧密耦合的动力学和不断变化的对手行为下做出快速决策。传统基于规则或微分博弈的方法难以应对高维空中交互和敏捷机动场景。

Method: AgilePE框架包含三部分：(1) 端到端策略网络，直接将机载状态观测映射为CTBR（集体推力和机体角速率）低层控制指令；(2) 竞争性自博弈训练，采用Prioritized Fictitious Self-Play (PFSP)结合多样化对手池，对抗历史策略以稳定优化并减少策略振荡；(3) 硬件对齐的仿真到实机部署管线，建模执行器响应动力学、通信延迟和域随机化，实现零样本迁移。

Result: 学习到的策略能零样本迁移到真实四旋翼而无需任务特定调参。真实世界实验再现了仿真中观察到的追逃战术（包括快速闪避和侧翼包抄），并展示了双智能体交互式零样本部署能力。

Conclusion: 通过将自博弈强化学习与端到端敏捷控制及硬件对齐仿真相结合，AgilePE在真实无人机上实现了仿真中涌现的复杂追逃策略，验证了该框架在解决高维、动态对抗性空中任务中的有效性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Autonomous pursuit-evasion is a fundamental challenge for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs), requiring rapid decision-making under tightly coupled dynamics and continuously changing opponent behaviors. Traditional rule-based or differential-game approaches often struggle with high-dimensional aerial interactions and agile maneuvering. We present AgilePE, a complete system for autonomous UAV pursuit-evasion via self-play reinforcement learning. AgilePE integrates agile low-level control, competitive policy optimization, and sim-to-real deployment in a unified framework. The policy directly maps onboard state observations to Collective Thrust and Body Rates (CTBR) commands, enabling end-to-end agile maneuvering without intermediate trajectory planners or waypoint controllers. For training, we use competitive self-play with Prioritized Fictitious Self-Play (PFSP) and a diversified opponent pool, enabling agents to improve against historical policies while stabilizing optimization and reducing policy oscillation. This process leads to the emergence of sophisticated pursuit and evasion strategies. For real-world deployment, we develop a hardware-aligned simulation pipeline that models actuator-response dynamics, communication latency, and domain randomization. The learned policies transfer zero-shot to real quadrotors without task-specific tuning. Real-world experiments reproduce pursuit-evasion tactics observed in simulation, including rapid dodging and flanking, and demonstrate interactive two-agent zero-shot deployment.

</details>


### [81] [OccPlanner: Goal-Aware Occupancy-Conditioned Diffusion Planner for Pixel-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14160)
*Binling Huang,Nianjin Ye,Xi Yang,Liang Hu,Zhou Huang,Shuang Wei,Longrui Yang,Yanchi Chen,Lanpeng Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OccPlanner，一种目标感知的占用条件扩散规划器，用于像素级目标导航。它将像素目标在自我中心度量空间中接地，并结合时序视觉上下文与局部3D占用特征。同时提出L3ROcc方法，从单目RGB导航视频中生成机器人中心局部3D占用标注。在5-8米设定下，平均成功率从20.81%提升至71.55%，并在真实机器人上验证了仿真到现实的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素级目标导航直接在智能体相机视图中指定目标，但目标像素既缺乏度量深度信息也不提供可通行性，这使得3D目标接地和避碰的连续规划变得困难。现有的方法无法有效解决这一核心问题。

Method: 提出OccPlanner（目标感知占用条件扩散规划器），核心做法包括：(1) 将像素目标在自我中心的度量空间中进行3D接地；(2) 在时序视觉上下文和学习到的局部3D占用特征上顺序条件化目标表示；(3) 提出L3ROcc，通过几何重建和基于射线的可见性推理，将单目RGB导航视频转换为机器人中心的局部3D占用标注，以在大规模数据上提供占用监督。

Result: 在InternData-N1上训练，在InternScenes的四个未见场景类别和两个目标距离范围上进行闭环仿真评估。在5-8米设定下，OccPlanner将平均成功率（SR）从20.81%（NavDP）提升至71.55%；在cluttered-easy和cluttered-hard场景中分别达到86.20%和84.92%。在Unitree Go2上的真实开放环路实验初步验证了仿真到现实的迁移及对L3ROcc监督的自适应能力。

Conclusion: OccPlanner通过将占用信息注入扩散规划过程，有效解决了像素目标导航中缺乏深度与可通行性信息的问题，在仿真和真实场景中都显示出显著的性能提升，证明了占用条件化方法在目标导航中的有效性及L3ROcc数据生成范式的可扩展性。

Abstract: Pixel-goal navigation specifies targets directly in the agent's camera view, but a target pixel provides neither metric depth nor traversability, making 3D goal grounding and collision-free continuous planning challenging. We present OccPlanner, a goal-aware occupancy-conditioned diffusion planner that grounds pixel goals in egocentric metric space and sequentially conditions the goal representation on temporal visual context and learned local 3D occupancy features. To provide occupancy supervision at scale, we introduce L3ROcc, which converts monocular RGB navigation videos into robot-centric local 3D occupancy annotations through geometric reconstruction and ray-based visibility reasoning. We train OccPlanner on InternData-N1 and evaluate it in closed-loop simulation across four unseen scene categories from InternScenes and two goal-distance ranges. In the 5-8 m setting, OccPlanner increases the average success rate (SR) over NavDP from 20.81% to 71.55% across the four categories, reaching 86.20% and 84.92% in cluttered-easy and cluttered-hard scenes, respectively. Real-world open-loop experiments on a Unitree Go2 further provide initial evidence of sim-to-real transfer and adaptation with L3ROcc-generated supervision.

</details>


### [82] [MMUSV-Sim: A Perception-Oriented Simulation and Data-Generation Platform for Multi-USV Cooperative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14207)
*Ziao Li,Jianxiong Ye,Biao Tang,Leping Zhang,Kun Zuo,Siyu Huang,Chenqiang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MMUSV-Sim，一个基于Unreal Engine 5和Project AirSim构建的面向多无人水面艇（USV）协同感知的仿真与数据生成平台。该平台提供岛屿、开放海域和港口等环境，支持可配置的天气、时间、波浪条件以及多样化的船舶资产和样条多艇运动，并同步采集RGB、深度、语义、LiDAR和雷达等多种模态数据。在生成的LiDAR协同BEV船舶检测实验中，Early Fusion达到AP@0.5为72.74，显著优于单USV的45.54。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多USV协同感知可通过融合互补观测扩展海上目标感知范围，但大规模开发此类系统需要统一的仿真平台来支持可配置的多USV场景、多模态数据采集和共享标注，目前缺乏这样的工具。

Method: 基于Unreal Engine 5和Project AirSim构建MMUSV-Sim平台，提供岛屿、开放海域、港口环境；可配置天气、时间、波浪条件；丰富的船舶资产库和基于样条的多艇运动模型。平台可同时从多个USV采集RGB、深度、语义、LiDAR和雷达观测，并捕获共同世界状态以实现逐代理标注导出。

Result: 实验验证了配置的波浪设置能产生预期的船舶升降、横摇和纵摇变化，并评估了投影标注与语义渲染之间的几何一致性。在LiDAR协同BEV船舶检测中，Early Fusion的AP@0.5为72.74，而单USV仅为45.54。

Conclusion: MMUSV-Sim为多USV协同感知研究提供了统一的仿真与数据生成工作流，实验表明多艇协同检测相比单艇有显著性能提升，验证了该平台的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Cooperative perception among multiple unmanned surface vehicles (USVs) combines complementary observations to extend maritime target sensing beyond the view range and field of a single platform. Developing such systems at scale calls for a unified workflow for configurable multi-USV scenarios, multimodal acquisition, and shared annotations. We present MMUSV-Sim, a perception-oriented maritime simulation and data-generation platform built on Unreal Engine 5 and Project AirSim. It provides island, open-sea, and port environments; configurable weather, time of day, and wave conditions; a diverse vessel asset library; and spline-based multi-vessel motion. MMUSV-Sim acquires RGB, depth, semantic, LiDAR, and radar observations across multiple USVs and captures a common world state for per-agent annotation export. Experiments verify that the configured wave settings produce the intended changes in vessel heave, roll, and pitch, and evaluate the geometric consistency between projected annotations and semantic renderings. In LiDAR-based cooperative BEV vessel detection experiments on the generated multi-USV dataset, Early Fusion achieves an AP@0.5 of 72.74, compared with 45.54 using a single USV.

</details>


### [83] [Vibration Suppression in Collaborative Flexible Payload Manipulation Using Passive Force Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14244)
*Alaa Abderrahim,Antonio Rosales,Ferdinando Milella,Markku Suomalainen,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于柔性强载荷协同搬运中抑制横向振动的控制策略。采用主从（leader-follower）构型的两台工业机械臂，主臂以整形速度指令引导运动，从臂通过导纳控制器对主臂施加的外部力保持柔顺，从而抑制柔性载荷的横向振动。通过等效质量-弹簧-阻尼模型建模，证明了系统的被动能量耗散特性，并通过数值仿真和实验结果验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型重型结构中，运动过程中会产生振动，这对精确操作构成重大挑战。在尖端项目中，如未来聚变能反应堆（托卡马克）的远程维护，此类柔性结构的操控被定义为关键任务。现有方法难以处理更重、更大的柔韧物体，且面临异构机器人规格、振动抑制等多重挑战。

Method: 采用主从（leader-follower）配置协同搬运策略：主臂通过整形的速度指令引导运动，从臂通过导纳控制器对主臂施加在载荷上的外部力保持柔顺。利用等效质量-弹簧-阻尼模型对主从载荷系统进行动力学建模，并证明在适当的导纳参数下系统总能量被被动耗散，同时提供了稳定性证明。

Result: 数值仿真验证了所提方法的正确性，实验结果证明了该控制策略的有效性，能够有效抑制柔性载荷在运动过程中的横向振动。

Conclusion: 所提出的主从协同搬运控制策略能够有效抑制柔韧性载荷的横向振动，实现了更重、更大柔性物体的协同操作，解决了异构机器人规格差异等挑战，为大型柔性结构的高精度操控提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: In large and heavy structures, vibrations arise during motion, posing significant challenges for precise manipulation. To accomplish the desired motion, control algorithms must effectively suppress these structural vibrations. In cutting edge projects, such as remote maintenance of future fusion energy reactors (tokamaks), the manipulation of this type of structure is defined as a crucial task. This paper presents a control strategy to suppress transverse vibrations in flexible payloads during motion using a collaborative payload manipulation approach. Two different industrial robot arms are arranged in a leader follower configuration for the manipulation strategy. The leader robot guides the motion with shaped velocity commands, while the follower robot ensures compliance with the estimated external forces applied by the leader on the payload through an admittance controller. Unlike existing methods, the proposed approach enables collaborative manipulation of heavier and larger flexible objects, addressing additional challenges such as vibration suppression and heterogeneous robot specifications. The dynamics of the leader follower payload system are modeled using an equivalent mass spring damper model, and it is shown that, with appropriate admittance parameters, the total energy of the system is passively dissipated. A stability proof is also provided. Numerical simulations validate the proposed method, and experimental results demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [84] [Accelerating Large-scale Bundle Adjustment for LiDAR Mapping via Parallel Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14266)
*Yixi Cai,Rundong Li,Yuhan Xie,Qingwen Zhang,Patric Jensfelt,Fu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个全并行计算框架来加速大规模LiDAR束调整（BA），通过自适应异步数据加载、自底向上体素化平面特征提取以及基于MM（majorization-minimization）公式的并行优化三大关键技术，在多个公开数据集上实现高达10倍的计算效率提升，同时保持与最先进方法相当的建图精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR束调整在大规模建图中至关重要，但现有方法在GPU上并行化程度不足，计算效率低下，难以满足大规模、高精度建图的需求。作者旨在提出一个完全并行的计算框架，突破当前LiDAR BA在内存受限GPU上处理大规模点云数据时的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出全并行LiDAR束调整框架，包含三项关键技术：(1) 自适应异步数据加载策略，在内存受限GPU上高效处理大规模点云；(2) 自底向上的体素化方法用于平面特征提取，实现全并行预处理；(3) 基于majorization-minimization（MM）公式加速优化过程中的计算密集型任务，包括残差、Jacobian、Hessian矩阵的计算及并行增量求解器。同时提供时间复杂度理论和实验分析。

Result: 在多种计算平台上对大规模公开数据集的广泛基准测试验证了方法的鲁棒性和适应性，计算效率最高提升达10倍，同时建图精度与最先进方法相当。

Conclusion: 该全并行LiDAR束调整框架有效解决了大规模建图中的计算效率问题，兼顾了性能与精度，并将实现代码开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: LiDAR bundle adjustment is widely utilized in mapping to construct globally consistent point cloud maps. In this paper, we propose the first fully parallel computing framework to accelerate LiDAR bundle adjustment for large-scale mapping, incorporating three key techniques. First, we design an adaptive, asynchronous data loading strategy to efficiently process large-scale point cloud datasets on memory-constrained GPUs. Secondly, we present a novel bottom-up voxelization method for extracting planar features, enabling fully parallelized pre-processing. Thirdly, we build upon a majorization-minimization formulation to accelerate compute-intensive tasks in the optimization via parallel computation, including the computation of residuals, Jacobian and Hessian matrices, and a parallel increment solver. To support our design, we provide both theoretical and experimental analysis of the time complexity of our approach. Extensive benchmarking on large-scale public datasets across various computational platforms validates the robustness and adaptability of our approach, achieving up to a tenfold improvement in computational efficiency while preserving mapping accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art methods. To benefit future research, the implementation code is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [85] [PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5: A Toolkit for Robot Process Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14284)
*Yuyang Liu,Yanqing Shen,Ruike Chen,Jifan Zhao,Yuxuan Tian,Yichi Zhang,Tianfeng Long,Zixuan Yin,Yipu Wang,Ziheng Qin,Wenxing Tan,Yang Shi,Mingyu Cao,Runze Xiao,Ziqi Wang,Zhixin Yin,Shiwei Chu,Yi-Fan Zhang,Yao Mu,Yuheng Ji,Yihao Wang,Jun Yan,Zhongyuan Wang,Pengwei Wang,Xiaolong Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5，一个用于机器人过程评估的工具包，通过将滚动视频转化为密集进度曲线并衍生多种细粒度指标，提供超越二元成功率和规则性过程分数的评估方法，同时引入RoboPulse++验证过程奖励模型可靠性，并发布完整的评估套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人评估依赖二元成功率和基于规则的过程分数，无法细粒度理解具身模型的真实能力，需要更精细、过程导向的评估方法。

Method: 提出PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5工具包，包含三个新指标（失败侧进度、回撤后恢复、成功侧执行质量），结合RoboPulse++验证过程奖励模型可靠性，并发布涵盖基准测试、指标实现和可视化工具的可复现评估套件。

Result: 基于多基准的滚动视频对具身模型进行全面评估，提供细粒度指标结果和关键发现，并用RoboPulse++为评估者提供更准确的过程奖励模型测试平台。

Conclusion: 呼吁社区重新思考机器人评估方式，将透明、程序化、可复现的评估作为下一代具身智能的基础。

Abstract: Fine-grained robotic evaluation matters for understanding embodied models, going beyond binary success rates and rule-based process scores. We present PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5, a toolkit for robot process assessment that turns rollout videos into dense progress curves and derives multiple fine metrics. PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5 introduces three metrics, building on version 1.0, that characterize failure-side progress, post-drawdown recovery, and success-side execution quality, helping users understand embodied model capability. Based on the rollout videos from benchmarks, we perform a comprehensive assessment of the embodied models, providing some fine-grained metric results and key findings. We further introduce RoboPulse++ to evaluate the reliability of process reward models (PRM), providing evaluators with a more accurate testing platform. Moreover, we release a user-friendly assessment suite, including the benchmark, metric implementation, and visualization tools, to support reproducible manipulation process evaluation. We call on the community to rethink how robots are evaluated and establish transparent, procedural, and reproducible assessment as a foundation for the next generation of embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [86] [CORAL: Curriculum-Optimized Reward Adaptation for LiDAR-Based Goal-Directed Urban Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14332)
*Anisa Saleem,Duksu Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CORAL是一种用于自动驾驶城市导航的强化学习方法，通过联合推进五阶段课程学习（逐步延长路线并收紧行为约束）和阶段感知奖励（各奖励分量权重随任务难度动态调整），在CARLA环境中使用PPO训练多流Actor-Critic网络。在长距离高约束任务上达到100%成功率，远超基线（5%和10%），并可零样本迁移到七个未见城镇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长距离目标导向的自动驾驶导航需要策略同时获取多种竞争性行为（到达远端目标、跟踪路线、避障、遵守信号），而固定目标函数缺乏学习这些行为的顺序，导致训练困难。

Method: 提出CORAL框架，包含两个联合调度的机制：(1) 五阶段课程学习，渐进式延长路线并收紧行为约束；(2) 阶段感知奖励，其组件权重从任务推进转向路线跟随、安全、平滑和规则合规。策略为多流Actor-Critic网络，使用PPO在CARLA中训练，输入为99维紧凑状态（极坐标LiDAR直方图、车辆遥测、自车框架路线几何、交通规则指示），无需点云编码器或鸟瞰图栅格化。

Result: 在最长路线且全部行为约束下的20个评估回合中，CORAL全部到达目标（100%），而两个PPO基线的成功率仅5%和10%。因子消融实验显示单一调度都不及两者组合：移除任一调度会同时降低成功率和路线完成度，同时禁用则成功率降至55%。在一个城镇训练的模型零样本迁移到七个未见城镇，在100-150米同等长度路线上成功率68-98%，平均横向偏差低于0.35米。

Conclusion: 课程学习与阶段感知奖励的协同对于解决长距离自动驾驶导航中多竞争行为的学习顺序问题至关重要；CORAL展示了高效、紧凑状态表示和良好泛化能力，为城市自主驾驶的RL方法提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is promising for autonomous urban driving, but long-horizon goal-directed navigation asks a policy to acquire several competing behaviors at once--reaching a distant goal, tracking a route, avoiding obstacles, obeying signals--and a fixed objective gives no order in which to learn them. This paper presents CORAL, which advances two schedules together: a five-stage curriculum that progressively lengthens routes and tightens behavioral constraints, and a stage-aware reward whose component weights shift emphasis from mission progress toward route following, safety, smoothness, and rule compliance as the task hardens. The policy is a multi-stream actor-critic network trained with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) in CARLA on a compact 99-dimensional state pairing a polar LiDAR histogram with vehicle telemetry, ego-frame route geometry, and traffic-rule indicators--no point-cloud encoder, no bird's-eye-view rasterization. Against two PPO baselines under an identical protocol, CORAL reaches the goal in all twenty evaluation episodes on the longest routes under the full set of behavioral constraints, where the baselines reach 5% and 10%; a factorial ablation shows that neither schedule alone matches their combination: removing either lowers both success and route completion, and disabling both drops success to 55%. Trained in one town, the policy transfers zero-shot to seven unseen towns, succeeding in 68-98% of episodes on routes of the same 100-150 m length, with mean lateral deviation below 0.35 m.

</details>


### [87] [Reflex: Enabling Fast and Predictive Vision-Language-Action Models for Reaction-Critical Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14379)
*Yuxuan Chen,Wanruo Zhang,Xiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出ReflexBench基准测试平台（包含六个动态操作任务，并可解耦模拟器步进与机器人控制以评测不同延迟下的反应能力），以及ReflexVLA模型——一个无需大规模机器人数据预训练的高效VLA模型，通过潜在未来预测和多帧时间融合增强时序推理，并利用批处理视觉编码和CUDA Graph重放降低部署延迟。实验显示ReflexVLA在动态操作任务上持续提升，同时保持静态操作基准的竞争力，真实世界实验也验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型基准主要评估静态操作任务的泛化能力，忽略了动态交互场景中对反应速度至关重要的需求。作者旨在填补这一评估空白，并解决VLA模型在反应关键时刻面临的高延迟和时序推理不足问题。

Method: 1) 构建ReflexBench：包含六个动态操作任务，提出将模拟器步进与机器人控制解耦的评估框架，支持同步和异步推理下可配置延迟的设置。2) 提出ReflexVLA：在视觉骨干中引入潜在未来预测与多帧时间融合增强时序推理；通过批处理视觉编码和CUDA Graph重放降低部署延迟，无需大规模机器人数据预训练。

Result: ReflexVLA在动态操作任务上持续优于基线模型，同时在标准静态操作基准上保持有竞争力的准确性；真实世界实验进一步验证了其在实际部署条件下的有效性。

Conclusion: 时序推理能力与推理延迟是反应关键时刻VLA模型性能的决定性因素。ReflexBench为动态操作评估提供了规范框架，ReflexVLA证明了可通过架构设计与工程优化，在不依赖大规模预训练的情况下显著提升反应式操作能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently achieved promising performance in robotic manipulation. However, existing benchmarks mainly evaluate generalization on static manipulation tasks and largely overlook dynamic interaction scenarios. To address this gap, we present ReflexBench, a benchmark for reaction-critical manipulation. ReflexBench contains six dynamic tasks and introduces an evaluation framework that decouples simulator stepping from robot control while supporting configurable latency under synchronous and asynchronous inference. Building upon ReflexBench, we propose ReflexVLA, an efficient VLA model designed for reaction-critical manipulation without large-scale robot-data pretraining. ReflexVLA enhances temporal reasoning through latent future prediction and multi-frame temporal fusion within the vision backbone, while reducing deployment latency through batched visual encoding and CUDA Graph replay. Experiments show that ReflexVLA consistently improves dynamic manipulation performance while maintaining competitive accuracy on standard static manipulation benchmarks, and real-world experiments further demonstrate its effectiveness under practical deployment conditions. Project website: https://reflexvla.github.io

</details>


### [88] [Effect of Twisted-Yarn Architecture on Pressure and Proximity Sensing Characteristics of Textile Capacitive Sensors for Robotic Skin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14406)
*Ishtia Zahir,Eslam Saleh,Maryam Rezayati,Güunter Grabher,Gaffar Hossain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于银包覆纱线和PDMS涂层的纺织集成电容传感平台，通过一、二、四层加捻结构系统研究纱线级架构对电容传感特性的影响。性能随层数提升而增强，四层传感器在100 kHz下灵敏度达0.1331 MPa⁻¹，具备低迟滞、低热漂移和15000次循环稳定性；同时实现60/50/40 mm的分层接近探测距离，并成功集成4×4传感阵列和机器人手臂进行实时触觉与接近检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纺织集成电容传感器具有柔性、保形等优势，可用于可穿戴电子和机器人交互，但纱线级架构（如加捻层数、电极重叠面积、纤维间距）对电容传感特性的影响尚未被充分量化，缺乏系统的架构-性能关联研究。

Method: 采用银包覆纱线涂覆PDMS，组装为一层、两层和四层加捻结构；系统性考察电极有效重叠面积和纤维间分离对电容响应的影响；通过压力计算（基于局部单纤维接触面积）、拉伸力学测试、灵敏度/迟滞/热漂移/循环稳定性评估以及接近探测实验建立架构与传感性能的定量关系。

Result: 随层数增加，力学性能提升（断裂伸长率37.5%→85.0%，最大载荷23.3→89.7 N）；灵敏度随层数和频率升高，四层传感器在100 kHz下达0.1331 MPa⁻¹；低迟滞、25-90°C热漂移极小、15000次循环稳定；接近探测范围随层数递减（60/50/40 mm），呈现架构依赖的灵敏度-探测范围权衡；4×4阵列实现空间接触映射，机器人集成系统端到端延迟403 ms。

Conclusion: 纱线加捻架构被确认为纺织集成电容传感系统中可调的关键设计参数，能够有效调控传感机理相关的力学、电容及探测性能，为纺织电容传感器的架构优化和功能定制提供了系统依据。

Abstract: Textile-integrated capacitive sensors offer flexible and conformable tactile sensing for wearable electronics and human-robot interaction; however, the influence of yarn-level architecture on capacitive transduction characteristics remains insufficiently quantified. This work presents a textile capacitive sensing platform based on silver-coated yarns coated with polydimethylsiloxane and assembled into one-, two-, and four-layer twisted configurations. The influence of effective electrode overlap area and inter-fiber separation on the capacitive response is systematically investigated, enabling architecture-dependent tuning of pressure and proximity sensing characteristics. Pressure was calculated using the localized single-fiber contact area, corresponding to stresses of 0.4-3.9 MPa. Increasing the layer number improved mechanical strength and sensing performance: elongation at break increased from 37.5% to 62.5% and 85.0%, while the maximum load increased from 23.3 to 42.7 and 89.7 N. Sensitivity increased with layer number and frequency, reaching 0.1331 MPa$^{-1}$ for the four-layer sensor at 100 kHz. The four-layer configuration also exhibited low hysteresis, minimal thermal drift from 25 to 90 $^\circ$C, and stable operation over 15,000 cycles. Proximity detection ranges of 60, 50, and 40 mm were obtained for the one-, two-, and four-layer sensors, respectively, revealing an architecture-dependent sensitivity-range trade-off. A 4$\times$4 textile sensing array enabled spatial contact mapping, while robotic-arm integration demonstrated real-time touch and proximity detection with an end-to-end robotic system latency (from detection to robot reaction) of 403 ms. The results establish yarn architecture as a tunable design parameter governing the measurement characteristics of textile-integrated capacitive sensing systems.

</details>


### [89] [Control-Informed Constraint Adaptation in Minimum-Time Trajectory Planning for Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14448)
*Ann-Kathrin Schwehn,Alexander Langmann,Mattia Piccinini,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种控制信息感知的在线轨迹规划框架，通过实时测量系统跟踪偏差并动态调整空间轨道约束，迭代扩展自由空间规划区域，使自主赛车在保持即时最优规划的同时补偿累积执行误差，在闭环仿真环境中将圈速降低1.8秒且计算负担不增（中位运行时间25ms）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主赛车在车辆动力学极限状态下运行，微小控制误差即导致安全关键行为与性能损失。现有轨迹规划器假设完美跟踪而对执行误差视而不见，为保障安全不得不采用保守的空间裕度，导致可用的赛道空间未被充分利用。

Method: 提出控制信息感知的在线轨迹规划框架。通过运行时测量系统性跟踪偏差，动态调整空间轨道约束，迭代扩展自由空间规划区域。规划器保持时间最优的同时补偿累积执行误差。在高保真闭环仿真环境中使用自主赛车进行分析验证。

Result: 该方法使圈速降低1.8秒且不增加计算负担，中位运行时间维持在25毫秒。

Conclusion: 将控制引起的偏差反馈至规划层，解锁了模块化架构此前无法企及的性能，使自主车辆能够系统性地利用赛道极限。

Abstract: Autonomous racecars operate at the limits of vehicle dynamics, where small control errors translate into safety-critical behavior and lost performance. Trajectory planners assume perfect tracking and remain blind to execution errors. To guarantee safety, trajectory planners therefore restrict themselves to conservative spatial margins, leaving usable track space untapped. To overcome these issues, we introduce a control-informed online trajectory planning framework that learns from its own execution errors. By measuring systematic tracking deviations during runtime, we dynamically adapt spatial track constraints and iteratively expand the free-space planning area. The planner remains time-optimal while compensating for accumulated execution errors. This method was analyzed in a high-fidelity closed-loop simulation environment with autonomous racecars. The results demonstrate that our approach reduces lap time by 1.8\,s without increasing computational burden, maintaining a median runtime of 25 ms. Our finding indicates that feeding control-induced deviations back into the planning layer unlocks performance previously inaccessible to modular architectures and enables autonomous vehicles to exploit track limits systematically.

</details>


### [90] [THRIVE: Therapeutic Humanoid Robot In Virtual Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14462)
*Jin Xu,Yu-Ping Chen,Ayanna Howard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: THRIVE是一个面向儿童上肢运动障碍的居家康复平台，整合了VR康复游戏、基于摄像头的实时运动追踪系统以及社交互动机器人治疗师。系统将治疗游戏与机器人实体解耦，采用模块化设计支持多种机器人形态，通过自适应动态反馈激励儿童并引导其运动朝向治疗目标，为家庭场景下持续、可扩展的上肢康复提供了实用路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童上肢运动障碍需要通过持续、任务导向的练习来改善功能，但传统康复方式在家庭环境中缺乏持续性、趣味性和可及性。现有康复系统往往绑定特定机器人硬件，成本高且难以扩展，限制了居家长期使用的可行性。

Method: THRIVE整合三个核心组件：1) 一套VR上肢康复游戏，涵盖弹出、击打、接住和抓取等任务，针对功能性伸展、抓握和物体操作动作进行训练；2) 基于摄像头的实时运动追踪系统，在游戏过程中捕获儿童的运动学表现；3) 社交互动机器人治疗师（可部署为实体机器人或远程临场虚拟代理），提供自适应动态反馈以激励和引导儿童。系统关键创新在于将治疗游戏与机器人实体解耦，实现机器人与形态无关的模块化架构。

Result: 该平台实现了游戏、运动追踪与机器人反馈的无缝集成，并通过机器人无关设计使系统具有低成本、可扩展性和对家庭环境的强适应性，支持多种机器人形态在同一模块化系统中的切换与部署。

Conclusion: THRIVE提供了一种实用且可扩展的家居康复路径，通过机器人与游戏解耦的模块化设计，有望促进儿童上肢运动障碍康复训练的持续性和参与度，为未来居家智能康复平台的发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper presents THRIVE (Therapeutic Humanoid Robot In Virtual Environment), an at-home rehabilitation platform that integrates a suite of virtual-reality upper-body rehabilitation games, a real-time camera-based motion-tracking system, and a socially interactive robot therapist. The system is designed for therapy and intervention in children with upper-limb motor impairments, which can be improved through consistent, task-specific practice. THRIVE features a set of newly designed, engaging games that target functional reaching, grasping, and object-manipulation movements through customizable popping, hitting, catching, and grabbing tasks, while the camera-based tracking system captures the child's kinematic performance during play. A robot therapist - deployable either as a physical robotic coach or as a remote-presence virtual agent - delivers adaptive, dynamic feedback to motivate the child and guide their movements toward therapeutic goals. THRIVE decouples the therapeutic games from the robot embodiment, extending the platform to support various embodiments and different robots within one modular system. This robot-agnostic design makes THRIVE affordable, scalable, and readily adaptable for sustained use in the home, offering a practical pathway to more consistent and engaging upper-limb therapy for children with motor function impairments.

</details>


### [91] [Expected Free Energy-based Informative Path Planning for Robotic Mars Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14466)
*Ajith Anil Meera,Pablo Lanillos,Wouter Kouw*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将主动推断中的期望自由能（EFE）作为统一的优化准则，用于解决预算受限的机器人信息路径规划问题，同时满足信息建图与高价值区域搜索两个目标。在硬路径长度约束下，基于高斯过程信念规划连续轨迹并最小化期望自由能，实验表明其优于信息论基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人在未知环境中探索时面临双重需求：既要构建准确的信息图，又要快速定位最高价值区域，同时还需控制每米行程和每次测量的成本。而传统的信息驱动和奖励驱动准则各自只解决其中一个目标，缺乏一个统一框架来同时兼顾信息获取与价值搜索。

Method: 将主动推断中的期望自由能（EFE）作为统一的行动选择目标函数，用于预算受限的机器人信息路径规划。具体而言，使用高斯过程来维护对信息场的信念，在硬路径长度约束下规划连续轨迹以最小化期望自由能，从而在不超出预算的前提下同时优化信息图精度和价值区域定位。

Result: 通过多个随机实现（realizations）的实验结果表明，基于EFE的规划方法能够同时生成准确的后验地图并定位最高价值区域，在相同设置下优于基于信息论的基线方法。

Conclusion: 期望自由能提供了一个统一、易于调参的原则性信息收集策略，在机器人探索中能够兼顾效率与资源约束，有助于促进自主部署。该框架为解决信息寻求与奖励寻求的矛盾提供了理论依据和实用方案。

Abstract: An autonomous robot efficiently exploring an unknown environment, such as looking for water sources on Mars, faces two simultaneous demands: building an accurate information map while quickly finding the regions of greatest value, and paying for every meter of travel and the cost of every measurement it takes. Classical information-seeking and reward-seeking criteria address only one of these objectives at a time. Here, we propose Expected Free Energy (EFE), the principled action-selection objective from active inference, as a unifying criterion for budgeted robotic informative path planning. Maintaining a Gaussian-process belief over the information field, our agent plans continuous trajectories that minimize expected free energy under hard path-length constraints. The results from multiple realizations show that EFE-based planning yields accurate posterior maps and locates the highest-value regions simultaneously, outperforming information-theoretic baselines under the same settings. In robotic exploration, these unified, easy-to-tune principled information-gathering strategies facilitate autonomous deployment while enforcing efficiency and resource constraints.

</details>


### [92] [Ensuring Safe Physical AI in Urban Mobility via Hazard-Informed Synthesized Envelopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14481)
*Alexei Odinokov,Rostislav Yavorskiy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，通过"危险信息感知的安全包络"（hazard-informed safety envelopes）将系统化危险分析与运行时执行相连接，采用跨层安全转换过程（涵盖符号、空间和动态世界模型），并与物理AI运行时封装接口，以确保异构机器人系统在城市环境中的安全移动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构机器人系统在多样化城市区域中部署时，复杂的人机交互环境下的安全保障仍是一个关键挑战。现有方法通常将安全视为孤立在单个软件模块中的静态约束，缺乏系统性、跨层的安全保证机制。

Method: 提出统一框架，核心是危险信息感知的安全包络，通过跨层安全转换过程将危险分析结果贯穿符号、空间和动态世界模型三个层面，并与物理AI运行时封装（runtime harnesses）自然对接，从而在运行时层面执行安全约束。

Result: 该框架成功实现了从系统化危险分析到运行时执行的桥接，展示了与物理AI运行时封装接口的兼容性，能够为城市移动安全提供保障。

Conclusion: 将安全从静态、模块孤立的约束转变为跨层、系统化的动态保障机制，能够在复杂人机交互场景中保证异构机器人的安全城市移动。

Abstract: As heterogeneous robotic systems deploy across diverse urban zones, maintaining safety amid complex human-robot interactions remains a critical challenge. We present a unified framework that bridges systematic hazard analysis and runtime enforcement using hazard-informed safety envelopes. Rather than treating safety as a static constraint isolated within individual software modules, we introduce a cross-layer safety transformation process spanning symbolic, spatial, and dynamic world models. We show how this representation naturally interfaces with physical AI runtime harnesses to guarantee safe urban mobility.

</details>


### [93] [Spatiotemporal Tube-Based Safety-Certificate for Autonomous Navigation of Articulated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14531)
*Mohd. Faizuddin Faruqui,Ratnangshu Das,Ravi Kumar L,Pushpak Jagtap*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时空管的规划方法，用于铰接式车辆（如牵引车-半挂车、拖曳AGV、公路列车等）的自主导航。该方法利用连接环节的运动学及摆动约束，为被驱动的牵引车生成安全管状路径，确保被拖曳的系列挂车始终保持在道路走廊内，并通过许可修正提供路径安全认证。该方法在复杂路线的卡车-挂车导航仿真中得到了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接式车辆是货运运输的主力，但其自主导航具有挑战性。其物理特性和运动约束使得在狭窄路线上操纵这些车辆十分困难，需要一种能够保证整列车队（包括拖曳的挂车链）始终保持在道路边界内的规划方法。

Method: 提出一种时空管（spatiotemporal tube）的方法：基于连接环节的运动学模型和摆动（sway）约束，为被驱动的牵引车（prime mover）构建一个安全的"管状"路径区域；然后利用许可修正（permissible corrections）对时空管进行修改，从而为车辆在给定路线上提供路径安全认证。

Result: 该方法在复杂的卡车-挂车导航仿真中得到了验证，表明能够提供认证的路径规划，确保拖曳的系列挂车始终保持在道路走廊内。

Conclusion: 所提出的时空管规划方法能够为铰接式车辆在复杂路线上提供经过认证的安全路径规划，通过运动学建模和摆动约束相结合，有效保证挂车车列不越出道路边界。

Abstract: Articulated vehicles are the workhorses of freight transportation, and their autonomous navigation is challenging. Their physical characteristics and motion constraints pose significant challenges in manoeuvring these vehicles on narrow routes. This paper presents a spatiotemporal tube-based approach to plan autonomous navigation of vehicles like tractor semi-trailers, truck/ tractor trailers, towing Automated Guided Vehicles (AGVs), and road trains. This planning approach provides a certified path plan for the truck or tractor, ensuring that the towed series of trailers always remains within the road corridor, limited by permissible corrections. The planning leverages the kinematics of the linked elements along with sway constraints to arrive at a safe tube for the actuated prime mover. We modify the spatiotemporal tube using permissible corrections to provide a route safety certificate to the vehicle for the given route. The proposed planning method is verified on a truck-trailer navigation simulation for a complex route.

</details>

### [94] [Inducing Reward-Free Judging Rubrics that Reduce Over-Crediting in Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13564)
*Darragh Quinn,David Dylan,Roisin Healy,Fionn Carroll,Maeve Donnelly,Cormac Sheehan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RubricForge通过反射进化方式，从少量带环境奖励标注的轨迹中自动生成可读的评分标准（rubric），用于无奖励环境下的智能体评估。相比通用G-Eval评判器，其总体一致性无显著优势，但能将误判失败轨迹为成功的比率降低约一半，从而在部署场景中更为可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模评估语言模型智能体时，常依赖第二个语言模型作为自动评判器，因为真实环境奖励信号昂贵、缓慢或在部署时不可用。然而现有评判器要么手写评分标准（如G-Eval），要么微调评判器权重，两者都倾向于把流畅但不成功的轨迹误判为成功。需要一个可信任、无需奖励信号的评判器。

Method: 提出RubricForge方法：从一小批带真实标注的轨迹中，通过反射进化（reflective evolution）迭代演化评判器文本评分标准，以最大化与环境奖励的一致性；随后冻结该标准，在单次模型调用中应用于留出轨迹，无需访问环境。优化产物为人类可读的文本，每条判定都可归因于具名标准。

Result: 在tau-bench（220次回滚中取173条标注轨迹）和WebShop（160条）上，使用同一个冻结的7B模型作为智能体和评判器，主要增益是忠实性而非原始一致率。相比G-Eval通用评判器，整体一致率无显著差异（McNemar p=0.248），绝对分数校准略微逊色（误差差-0.048，p=2x10^-4）。但RubricForge将失败轨迹误判为成功的比率约降低一半（tau-bench上0.115 vs 0.173），且对WebShop结果的等级评定更忠实（Spearman 0.410 vs 0.370）。

Conclusion: 对无需奖励的评判器而言，误判通过率（false-pass rate）而非总体一致率才是部署相关的关键指标：误判通过会放行有缺陷的智能体，而误判失败仅需重试一次。RubricForge的文本化、可归因的评分标准在降低误判通过率方面具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Evaluating language-model agents at scale increasingly relies on a second language model as an automatic judge, because the gold signal, an executable environment reward, is expensive, slow, or unavailable at deployment time. Such a judge is a reward-free proxy whose value depends on whether it can be trusted, yet existing judges either hand-write the scoring rubric, as in G-Eval, or fine-tune the judge's weights, and both tend to credit fluent but unsuccessful trajectories as successes. We instead induce the text of an agent-judging rubric from a small set of ground-truth-labeled trajectories, grounding it in true outcomes. We present RubricForge, which evolves a judge rubric by reflective evolution against labeled trajectories to maximize agreement with the environment reward, freezes it, and applies it to held-out trajectories in one model call with no environment access. The optimized artifact is human-readable text, so every verdict is attributable to named criteria. Using one frozen 7B model as both agent and judge, on tau-bench (173 labeled trajectories drawn from 220 rollouts) and WebShop (160), the principal gain is faithfulness rather than raw agreement. The edge over a generic G-Eval judge is not statistically significant (McNemar p = 0.248), and absolute-score calibration marginally favors the generic judge (|err| difference -0.048, p = 2x10^-4). Yet RubricForge over-credits failed trajectories roughly half as often (0.115 vs. 0.173 false-pass rate on tau-bench, with three over-credit catches and zero reversals) and ranks graded WebShop outcomes more faithfully (Spearman 0.410 vs. 0.370). For a reward-free evaluator the false-pass rate, not aggregate agreement, is the deployment-relevant quantity, since a false pass ships a broken agent whereas a false fail merely costs a retry.

</details>


### [95] [Depth-Aware Sensitivity Analysis of Mixture-of-Experts Models via Magnitude-Based Expert Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13565)
*Pradeep Kumar Sharma,Shantanu Godbole,Hritvik Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) while preserving computational efficiency through sparse activation. Despite their widespread adoption, the relative importance of individual MoE layers remains insufficiently characterized, particularly for model compression. This paper presents a systematic layer-wise sensitivity analysis of the Qwen3.6-35B-A3B model (40 MoE layers, 256 experts per layer, top-8 routing) using magnitude-based expert masking on the XLCoST cross-lingual code translation benchmark. We conduct a multi-phase study spanning 100, 300, and 500 prompt evaluation scales across three H100 GPU servers. Our central finding is that layer sensitivity is strongly depth-dependent: early layers (0-9) and middle layers (10-29) are highly fragile to expert masking, while late layers (30-39), and especially very-late layers (35-39), tolerate aggressive masking of low-magnitude experts. Flat all-layer masking at 30% retains only 150/300 Good+Similar outputs at 300-prompt scale, whereas late-focused policies retain 249-255/300 while masking 640-1,145 experts. On a later 500-prompt held-out validation slice, the narrow very-late policy (layers 35-39 @ 50%) achieves the strongest quality/masked-expert tradeoff among tested candidates, retaining 419/500 Good+Similar outputs while masking only 640 of 10,240 total experts. We additionally characterize top-k routing width reduction from 8 to 6 active experts per token, which shows a large observed wall-clock reduction on a 100-prompt probe with no Good+Similar loss, though it does not yet compose cleanly with aggressive expert masking. These findings provide an empirical foundation for depth-aware MoE expert masking and establish a practical path toward physical weight surgery, activation-based expert scoring, and training-based recovery.

</details>


### [96] [Modular Cognitive Architecture Emerges in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13567)
*Pengrui Han,Jacob Andreas,Evelina Fedorenko,Andrea Gregor de Varda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过46项任务、四个认知域（语言、形式推理、社会推理、物理推理）的电路分析，发现LLM中涌现出与人类大脑功能模块化相似的架构：人类使用相同网络的任务在LLM中激活重叠神经元，不同网络的任务则激活不同神经元，表明模块化可能是智能系统的普适特征而非生物大脑的进化偶然。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类大脑展现高度功能专门化，不同网络分别支持语言、形式推理、心理推理和物理推理。一个关键问题是：这种模块化组织是智能系统必须遵循的基本原理，还是仅存在于生物大脑的进化偶然？通过检验另一类通过完全不同优化过程产生的智能系统（LLM）是否涌现类似组织，可回答此问题。

Method: 对N=46个任务进行电路分析（circuit analyses），任务横跨四个认知域：语言、形式推理、社会推理和物理推理。比较LLM中神经元的激活模式与人类大脑网络的映射关系，考察同一认知域的任务是否在LLM中调用重叠神经元，不同认知域的任务是否调用不同神经元。

Result: LLM中出现了与人类大脑镜像对应的模块化架构：在人类中依赖相同网络的任务在LLM中招募重叠的神经元，而在人类中依赖不同网络的任务则招募不同的神经元。大脑与神经网络中模块化的趋同涌现得到验证。

Conclusion: 模块化组织在大脑和神经网络中的趋同涌现表明，功能模块化可能是智能系统的一种基本属性，而不仅是生物进化的特定产物。这一发现对理解通用智能的架构原理具有重要启示。

Abstract: The human brain exhibits a striking degree of functional specialization, with distinct networks supporting language, formal reasoning, reasoning about other minds, and reasoning about the physical world. Is this modular organization a fundamental principle of how intelligent systems must be built, or an evolutionary accident specific to biological brains? Here, we test whether a similar organization emerges in Large Language Models--another class of intelligent systems created through a very different optimization process. Using circuit analyses across N=46 tasks spanning four cognitive domains (language, formal reasoning, social reasoning, physical reasoning), we find that LLMs develop a modular architecture that mirrors the human brain: tasks drawing on the same network in humans recruit overlapping neurons in LLMs, whereas tasks drawing on different networks recruit distinct neurons. The convergent emergence of modularity in brains and neural networks suggests that it may be a fundamental property of intelligent systems.

</details>


### [97] [A Year in LLM Serving: Workload Evolution, Caching and Load-Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13573)
*William Nixon,Jon Durbin,Florian Standhartinger,Haryadi S. Gunawi,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对Chutes平台一年期生产环境trace的全局特征刻画和纵向研究，深入揭示了真实世界LLM推理服务负载的演变规律和用户-模型交互结构，并公开完整trace供后续研究使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务负载研究在规模和范围上受限，通常只观察短时间窗口，对生产环境中用户与模型的交互方式可见性不足，无法充分捕捉负载随时间的演变以及用户-模型交互对生产流量的塑造作用。需要更大规模、更长周期的真实trace来支撑LLM服务系统的激励和基准测试。

Method: 从Chutes平台收集为期一年的完整生产trace，涵盖多模型、多用户的全部生产行为（包括热门和长尾模型），从聚合、时间序列、模型级别和用户级别四个视角进行分析，揭示聚合视图下隐藏的负载演变规律和用户-模型结构。

Result: 从多个分析维度揭示了LLM服务负载的演变趋势和用户-模型交互结构，这些模式在传统聚合视图中难以观察。研究将公开一年期完整trace，支持下游研究使用真实生产数据。

Conclusion: 通过发布真实、全量、长期的生产trace并多维度分析，显著推进了对LLM服务负载的理解，为后续服务系统的设计和基准测试提供了可靠基础和公开数据资源。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) serving has become a critical cloud workload, and realistic traces are essential for motivating and benchmarking serving systems. However, existing LLM serving workload studies remain limited in scale and scope. They often observe short time periods and provide limited visibility into how users interact with models in production. As a result, they do not fully capture how LLM serving workloads evolve over time or how user-model interactions shape production traffic.
  In this work, we further the understanding of real-world LLM serving workloads through both a global characterization and a longitudinal study of a one-year production trace from Chutes. Unlike prior studies, our trace captures full production behavior across many models and users, including both popular and long-tail models. We analyze the workload from aggregate, temporal, model-level, and user-level perspectives, revealing workload evolution and user-model structure that are typically hidden behind aggregate views. To support future research, we will release the full one-year trace with the paper, enabling downstream studies of production behavior without relying on sampled or synthetically generated workloads.

</details>


### [98] [Agentao: A Governed Local-First Runtime for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13574)
*Bo Jin,Qiang Jiao,Xin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agentao是一个受治理的本地优先运行时系统，用于工具使用的LLM代理。它通过分层架构将模型生成的行动提案与主机授权的执行分离，将权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹作为显式运行时抽象，以构建更可治理、可检查、适合主机控制的本地环境的代理系统。代码已在GitHub公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理日益充当执行系统，能够调用工具、修改本地状态、使用持久记忆并与外部协议交互。这些能力引入了过度权限操作、弱可审计性、提示注入、工具投毒和不受控制的副作用等风险。论文旨在解决这些安全问题，提出一个更可治理和可检查的代理运行时架构。

Method: Agentao采用分层架构，将模型生成的行动提案与主机授权的执行相分离。架构包含：主机面向的接口层、主机契约、运行时核心、权限中介工具系统，以及支持记忆、重放、插件、技能、子代理和协议集成的子系统。系统通过结构化事件接口和显式的权限、状态、协议边界抽象来管理代理执行。

Result: Agentao展示了如何将权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹变为显式的运行时抽象。论文明确指出该系统不提供形式化的安全保证，而是通过架构设计演示如何构建更可治理、可检查和适合主机控制的本地环境的代理系统。代码已公开在GitHub仓库中。

Conclusion: Agentao通过分离行动提案与授权执行、显式化治理抽象的方式，为工具使用型LLM代理提供了一种可治理的本地优先运行时方案。它不声称提供正式的安全保证，而是通过分层架构和结构化事件接口，为构建受主机控制的、可审计和可检查的代理系统提供了实用的设计范式。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate as execution systems that invoke tools, modify local state, use persistent memory, and interact with external protocols. These capabilities make agents useful, but they also introduce risks related to over-privileged actions, weak auditability, prompt injection, tool poisoning, and uncontrolled side effects. This paper presents Agentao, a governed local-first runtime for tool-using LLM agents. Agentao separates model-generated action proposals from host-authorized execution through a layered architecture consisting of host-facing surfaces, a host contract, a runtime core, a permission-mediated tool system, and supporting subsystems for memory, replay, plugins, skills, sub-agents, and protocol integration. We describe the motivation, threat model, design goals, governance model, execution pipeline, and structured event interface of the system. Agentao does not provide formal safety guarantees; rather, it demonstrates how permissions, state, protocol boundaries, and execution traces can be made explicit runtime abstractions for building agents that are more governable, inspectable, and suitable for host-controlled local environments. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/jin-bo/agentao.

</details>


### [99] [AI Evaluation Should Work With Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13577)
*Jan Kulveit,Gavin Leech,Tomáš Gavenčiak,Raymond Douglas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出观点：当前AI评估范式过于关注超人自主表现，实质是以替代人为目标，这正将AI发展引向错误方向。作者主张应转向评估人机协作团队的表现，认为这种协作导向的转变能促使AI真正成为人类能力的补充，从而带来更好的社会结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估的主流范式（聚焦超人自主表现）隐含地将"替代人类"作为目标，这在作者看来将AI发展引向错误方向，可能导致不利的社会影响。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文（position paper），通过论证和推理提出AI评估范式应从"自主超人性能"转向"人机团队性能"评估。

Result: 主张AI评估应转向人机协作团队的表现评估，以此促使AI系统真正成为人类能力的补充（complements），而非替代者。

Conclusion: 协作导向的评估转变将引导AI朝着补充人类能力的方向发展，相比当前过程会带来更好的社会结果。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the dominant paradigm of AI evaluation (which focuses on superhuman autonomous performance and so implicitly targets the goal of replacing humans) is guiding AI development in the wrong direction. Instead, the AI community should pivot to evaluating the performance of human--AI teams. We argue that this collaborative shift will foster AI systems that act as true complements to human capabilities and therefore lead to far better societal outcomes than will the current process.

</details>


### [100] [Stable Miscalibration in Large Language Models: A Practical View of High-Confidence Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13591)
*Akira Okutomi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型中高置信度错误是否源于内部推理脆弱性，提出了另一种可能性：稳定性的错误校准（stable miscalibration），即高置信错误答案在局部扰动下保持稳定。研究结合了输出层审计评分与内部敏感性探针两种诊断方法，发现自批判提示能促进隐藏状态局部稳定化，但审计定义的高置信错误并不比置信正确的答案更局部敏感，表明部分高置信错误可能是稳定且错误校准的，而非单纯推理脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型中的高置信度错误通常被视为内部推理脆弱的证据。作者质疑这一假设，提出另一种可能性：某些高置信错误是稳定存在的错误校准现象，而非推理过程的不稳定。区分这两种机制对于理解模型行为、改进校准和决策系统至关重要。

Method: 结合两种诊断方法：(1) 标签感知的输出层审计评分，在强制答案基线下按置信度变化和过度自信错误对领域进行排序；(2) 内部敏感性探针，测量隐藏状态在扰动下的移动。在多领域二元事实审计集上，用三个开放权重模型在多个层上进行验证。

Result: 审计评分能追踪自批判减少决策损失的位置，但直接标记基线能更强地排名相同收益。自批判提示能一致地降低跨层隐藏状态敏感性，支持提示诱导的局部稳定化而非纯输出层弃权模式。但审计定义的高置信错误并不比置信正确的答案更局部敏感，说明部分高置信错误可能是稳定且错误校准的。

Conclusion: 高置信错误并不必然反映推理脆弱性；部分错误可能是稳定的错误校准现象。自批判提示通过内部机制而非单纯输出层模式起作用，但这不等同于校准。研究为区分"脆弱推理"与"稳定错误校准"提供了方法框架，揭示了LLM高置信错误的另一面。

Abstract: High-confidence errors in large language models are often treated as evidence of fragile internal inference. We study a different possibility: stable miscalibration, where a confident wrong answer remains locally stable under small perturbations. We combine two diagnostics: a label-aware output-level audit score that ranks domains by confidence variation and overconfident mistakes under a forced-answer baseline, and an internal sensitivity probe that measures hidden-state movement. On a multi-domain binary factual audit set, this audit score tracks where abstention-aware self-critique reduces decision loss, although direct labeled baselines rank the same gain more strongly. Internally, self-critical prompting consistently reduces hidden-state sensitivity across layers in three open-weight models. This supports prompt-induced local stabilization rather than a purely output-level abstention pattern, but it does not imply calibration: audit-defined overconfident errors are not clearly more locally sensitive than confidently correct answers, so some high-confidence errors may be stable and miscalibrated rather than simply fragile.

</details>


### [101] [Measuring Cross-Task Behavioral Consistency in Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13598)
*Amritesh Banerjee,Pranil Raichura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出行为一致性指标（BCM），通过分析智能体执行轨迹中行为特征对任务成功的归因向量，度量智能体跨任务与任务内的一致性。基于约9,000条条来自六个语言模型智能体的软件工程任务轨迹，发现跨任务一致性与任务内一致性是可分离的不同轴，且一致性无法由成功率等结果指标解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估几乎完全依赖成功率等结果指标，仅能反映智能体是否成功，而无法捕捉其行为的一致性与稳健性。以往工作仅测量同任务重复执行的可复现性，无法区分"局部可复现但全局碎片化"与"多尺度一致"的智能体系统，缺乏对行为一致性的系统化量化方法。

Method: BCM方法包含三个步骤：（1）训练一个模型，从智能体执行轨迹的行为特征预测任务成功；（2）为每条轨迹推导特征归因向量；（3）度量同一智能体系统内轨迹间归因向量的平均成对相似度。实验涵盖约9,000条轨迹，来自六个语言模型智能体在软件工程任务上的表现。

Result: 核心发现如下：跨任务一致性与任务内一致性是不同的轴，二者可能分离——某些系统在单任务重复尝试中行为相似（局部可复现），但跨任务缺乏稳定策略（全局碎片化）；一致性不可还原为成功率，成功率相当的智能体系统一致性可差异显著；在前沿模型与开源模型的一致性差距上，在控制任务难度的任务内条件下依然存在。

Conclusion: BCM作为过程层面的可靠性信号，可补充结果指标以更全面地评估智能体系统。作者明确指出BCM有意义的前提条件，并强调行为一致性是需要独立测度的属性。

Abstract: Agent evaluation relies almost entirely on outcome metrics such as success rate, which capture whether an agent succeeds but not how consistently it behaves. We argue that behavioral consistency across tasks is a distinct and measurable property, and we introduce the Behavioral Consistency Metric (BCM) to quantify it. BCM trains a model to predict task success from behavioral features of agent execution traces, derives a per-trajectory feature-attribution vector, and measures the mean pairwise similarity of these vectors within an agent system. Across roughly 9,000 trajectories from six language model agents on software engineering tasks, our central finding is that cross-task and within-task consistency are distinct axes that can diverge: some systems are locally reproducible, behaving similarly on repeated attempts at one task, yet globally fragmented, with no stable strategy across different tasks, while others are consistent at both scales. Prior work measures only same-task reproducibility and so cannot observe this separation. We further find that consistency is not reducible to success rate, since systems with comparable success can differ sharply in consistency, and that the frontier-versus-open-source consistency gap persists under a within-task control that holds task difficulty constant. We position BCM as a process-level reliability signal that complements outcome metrics, and we are explicit about the conditions under which it is meaningful.

</details>


### [102] [Cross-Disciplinary Taxonomy and Modeling of Misunderstanding Generation, Amplification, and Detection, from Pragmatics to AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13604)
*Babak Abbaschian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了AI介导沟通中误解检测的紧迫问题，将误解视为一个分层过程（分歧产生→放大→检测修复或持续未察），从九个相互独立的研究领域整合出11种确切的失败模式，归纳出8个分析层级，并对这些层级进行了形式化建模，扩展了信息与通信理论以涵盖意义重建而非仅信号传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着沟通从实时、面对面的交互转向AI介导渠道，沟通者失去了修复对话所依赖的线索资源，而新的误解检测手段尚未跟上，使误解检测成为一个亟待解决的问题。

Method: 本文采取多领域综合分析方法：整合九个通常互不引用的研究领域的观点，辨别出11种确切的失败模式；根据每种模式在沟通过程中的具体作用点归纳出8个分析层级（来源于文献而非沿用现有模型）；对8个层级进行形式化建模，扩展信息与通信理论从信号传输到意义重建；并提供源对源的证据矩阵、编码手册及9个对话分析案例以确保可审计性。

Result: 识别出11种失败模式，其中8种主要产生分歧、2种主要放大已存在的分歧、1种控制分歧是否被检测和修复；确定了8个基于文献的分析层级；完成了形式化建模并提供了证据矩阵、编码手册和9个对话案例。

Conclusion: 本文首次提出一种误解分类方法，既能将机制定位在沟通过程的具体节点上，又能按功能对其进行类型化，弥补了此前误解分类研究的空白。

Abstract: Detection of misunderstanding is an urgent problem to solve because communication has moved away from real-time, in-person interaction and is increasingly handled by AI-mediated channels. This shift cuts communicators off from the resources repair depends on faster than new means of detection are being built. In this paper we analyse misunderstanding as a layered process in which a divergence is generated, may then be amplified, and is either detected and repaired or left to persist unnoticed. Consolidating accounts from nine fields of research that do not ordinarily cite one another, we identify eleven exact failure modes and show that each operates at a specific point in a communicative process rather than anywhere within it. Those points give eight analytical layers, derived from the literature rather than adopted from an existing model. Eight of the mechanisms primarily generate a divergence, two primarily amplify one already present, and one governs whether a divergence is detected and repaired. We model the eight layers formally, extending information and communication theory from the transmission of signals to the reconstruction of meaning, and we supply a source-by-source evidence matrix that makes every rating auditable, a coding manual, and nine analysed dialogue cases.
